8e:Td47,<p><strong>The idea:</strong> two quantities are connected by an equation, and both are changing over time. You're told how fast one is changing and asked how fast the other is changing <em>at one particular moment</em>.</p>
<p>The key move: differentiate the equation <strong>with respect to time <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">t</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6151em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">t</span></span></span></span></strong>.
